## Multiblocks with unrecognized stat lines

### Absolute Baryonic Perfection Purification Unit  [maps: Baryonic Perfection]
     Absolute Baryonic Perfection Purification Unit
     Machine Type: Purification Unit
  ?? Water Tier: 8
     Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
     -----------------------------------------
     Insert Quark Releasing Catalysts into the input bus while running
     Every recipe cycle, a different combination of 2 different Quark Releasing Catalysts
     will correctly identify the lone quark and succeed the recipe
     The order of catalysts in the combination does not matter, so Up/Down can be identified by inserting Down/Up
     -----------------------------------------
     Every 20 ticks, consumes ALL catalysts in the input bus
     The base cost of inserting a catalyst is 144L Molten Infinity
     For every duplicate occurrence of an inserted catalyst in the sequence, this cost is doubled
     -----------------------------------------
     Keeps track of the entire sequence of catalysts inserted this recipe
     If the correct catalyst combination is in the sequence of inserted catalysts, immediately outputs 2000L Stabilised Baryonic Matter
  ?? At the end of a successful recipe, outputs additional 500L Stabilised Baryonic Matter per parallel
     At the end of the recipe, returns all incorrectly inserted catalysts in the output bus
     -----------------------------------------
     The final stage of water purification goes beyond subatomic particles and identifies the smallest
     possible imperfections within the baryons themselves. By correctly identifying which pairs of quark
     flavors are required, the unit will activate the catalysts, stabilizing the errant particles
     This ultimately creates both Stabilised Baryonic Matter and, most importantly, absolutely perfectly purified water.
     -----------------------------------------
     9
     shift

### Bose-Einstein Condensate Entanglement Apparatus  [maps: Condensate Entanglement]
     Bose-Einstein Condensate Entanglement Apparatus
     Machine Type: BEC Generator, Condensate Entangler, Input Hatch

### Eye of Harmony  [maps: Eye of Harmony]
     Eye of Harmony
     Machine Type: Spacetime Manipulator, EOH
     Creates a pocket of spacetime that is bigger on the inside using transdimensional
  ?? engineering. Certified Time Lord regulation compliant. This multi uses too much EU
  ?? to be handled with conventional means. All EU requirements are handled directly by
  ?? your wireless EU network
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     This multiblock will constantly consume hydrogen and helium when it is not running a
     recipe once per second. It will store this internally, you can see the totals by
  ?? using a scanner. This multi also has three tiered blocks with 9 tiers
     each. They are as follows and have the associated effects on the multi:
     Spacetime Compression Field Generator:
  ?? - The tier of this block determines what recipes can be run. If the multiblocks
     spacetime compression field block exceeds the requirements of the recipe it
  ?? will decrease the processing time by 3% per tier over the requirement (multiplicative)
     Time Dilation Field Generator:
  ?? - Decreases the time required for a recipe by 50% per tier of block (multiplicative)
  ?? Decreases the probability of a recipe succeeding by 9.25% per tier (additive)
     Stabilisation Field Generator:
  ?? - Increases the probability of a recipe succeeding by 5% per tier (additive)
  ?? Decreases the yield of a recipe by 5% per tier (additive).
  ?? > Low tier stabilisation field generators have a power output penalty
  ?? The power output penalty for using Crude Stabilisation Field Generators is 40%
  ?? This penalty decreases by 5% per tier (additive)
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ?? Going over a recipe requirement on hydrogen or helium has a penalty on yield and recipe chance
     All stored hydrogen and helium is consumed during a craft. The associated formulas are:
     Overflow ratio = (Stored fluid / Recipe requirement) - 1
     Adjustment value = 1 - exp(-(30 * Overflow ratio)^2)
  ?? The Adjustment value is then subtracted from the total yield and recipe chance
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ?? It should be noted that base recipe chance is determined per recipe and yield always starts
     at 1 and subtracts depending on penalties. All fluid/item outputs are multiplied by the
     yield. Failure fluid is exempt
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     This multiblock can only output to ME output buses/hatches
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ?? This multiblock can be overclocked by placing a programmed circuit into the input bus
  ?? E.g. A circuit of 2 will provide 2 OCs, 16x EU input and 0.25x the time. EU output is unaffected
  ?? All outputs are equal. All item and fluid output chances & amounts per recipe are unaffected
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ?? If a recipe fails the EOH will output Success chance * 14,400 * (2.0)^(Recipe tier)L of molten
  ?? SpaceTime instead of fluid/item outputs and output as much EU as a successful recipe
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ?? This multiblock can perform parallel processing by placing Astral Array Fabricators into the input bus
     They are stored internally and can be retrieved via right-clicking the controller with a wire cutter
  ?? The maximum amount of stored Astral Arrays is 8,637. Parallel amount is calculated via these formulas:
  ?? Parallel exponent = floor(log(8 * Astral Array amount) / log(1.7))
  ?? Parallel = 2^(Parallel exponent)
  ?? If the EOH is running parallel recipes, the power calculation changes
  ?? The power needed for parallel processing is calculated as follows:
  ?? total EU = ((EU output - EU input * 1.63) / 20.7) * 2.3^(Parallel exponent)
  ?? Furthermore, if parallel recipes are run, the recipes consume Condensed Raw Stellar Plasma Mixture
     instead of helium and hydrogen. Overflow penalties still apply
  ?? The required amount of fluid to start a recipe is 12.4 / 10^6 * Helium amount * Parallel
  ?? The success or failure of each parallel is determined independently
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     Animations can be disabled by using a screwdriver on the multiblock
     Planet block can be inserted directly by right-clicking the controller with planet block
     ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     Added by: Colen
     shift

     of aggregating dispersed suspended particles from a solution into larger clumps for further filtration.

### Large Hadron Collider  [maps: Large Hadron Collider]
     Large Hadron Collider
     Machine Type: Particle Accelerator, Particle Collider, LHC
     Accelerates particles to high energies and then collides them to generate new particles
     Electrically neutral particles are unaffected
     Set the Target Beam Energy (up to 2TeV) and the maximum number of cycles
     Cycles every second
     -----------------------------------------
     Accelerator mode
     Accelerates the beam in the smaller LHC ring
  ?? Accelerator mode increases the Beam Energy by EU Consumed in cycle * 0.1eV / Beam Rate,
     until the Target Beam Energy is reached, or the maximum number of cycles is reached
     If the Target Beam Energy is reached, add 0.3 * Beam Rate to the current Beam Rate
     However, if the current Beam Rate >= 5000, add (0.3 * Beam Rate)^0.5
     to the current Beam Rate instead
  ?? Power cost starts at 1A of UV per Beam Rate and increases quadratically
     with the number of completed cycles
     P = (1A UV) * R * (N+1)^2
     Automatically switches to Collider mode when the maximum number of cycles is reached
     -----------------------------------------
     Collider mode
     Splits the beam into two beams that go in opposite directions in the larger LHC ring, which
     then collide to generate new particles
     The Collision Energy is two times the Beam Energy
     If the Collision Energy exceeds the rest mass of a particle, that particle will start
     appearing in the outputs
     A particle output can only appear in the modules that correspond to the forces that
     interact with that particle
     For example, Neutrinos cannot appear in the Strong force module, ALICE, since they do
     not interact via the strong force
     Filtered Beam Output Hatches will allow you to see the list of possible outputs, and
     filter them
     -----------------------------------------
     There are four LHC modules, marked by the letter on top of the module:
     E - Electromagnetism - Charged Matter Sensor (CMS)
     W - Weak Interaction - Advanced Total Lepton Assimilation Snare (ATLAS)
     S - Strong Force - Absolute Lattice Integrated Chromodynamic Encapsulator (ALICE)
     G - Gravity - Localized Horizon Curvature Binder (LHCb)
     -----------------------------------------
     Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
     -----------------------------------------
     Added by: Author: HamCorp
     shift

### Nanochip Assembly Complex  [maps: Nanochip Conversion Complex]
     Nanochip Assembly Complex
     Machine Type: NAC, Nanochip Assembly Complex
  ?? Uses Circuit Components (CCs) to create circuits at unimaginable speeds
     Click the Logo Icon in the GUI for more information
     -----------------------------------------
     CCs can only exist in the perfect environment present in Vacuum Conveyor Hatches
     Convert items to CCs in the control room by placing them in a colored input bus
     Every 5 seconds, all items in colored input buses are placed
     in Vacuum Conveyor Outputs (VCOs) of the same color
     Finished circuit CCs are converted back into circuits by routing them to a VCI in the control room
     CCs in a colored VCI are placed in an output bus of the same color
     -----------------------------------------
     CCs must be processed into a refined version by modules before they
     can be assembled into a circuit in the Nanochip Assembly Matrix
  ?? All power from the energy hatch is distributed to the modules as they need it

### pH Neutralization Purification Unit  [maps: pH Neutralization]
     pH Neutralization Purification Unit
     Machine Type: Purification Unit
  ?? Water Tier: 4
     Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
     -----------------------------------------
     Initial pH value every cycle varies from 4.5 - 9.5 pH
     If the pH value is within 0.05 pH of 7.0 pH at the end of the cycle, the recipe always succeeds
     Otherwise, the recipe always fails
     Use a pH Sensor Hatch to read the current pH value
     For safety, the machine will shut down if the pH goes below 0 or exceeds 14
     -----------------------------------------
     Every 20 ticks, consumes ALL Sodium Hydroxide and Hydrochloric Acid in the special hatches
     Raises the pH value by 0.01 pH per piece of Sodium Hydroxide Dust
     Lowers the pH value by 0.01 pH per 10L of Hydrochloric Acid
     -----------------------------------------
     The fourth step of water purification is to neutralize the solution and bring its pH to exactly 7, rendering
     the solution inert with no hydrogen ion activity beyond water’s natural amphiproticity. Acids and bases from soils
     and geology cause natural alkalinity variations in water which can cause corrosive reactions with sensitive
     materials. This necessitates the use of the corresponding neutralizing agents to pH balance the water.
     -----------------------------------------
     5
     shift

### Pseudostable Black Hole Containment Field  [maps: Compressor, Neutronium Compressor]
     Pseudostable Black Hole Containment Field
     Machine Type: Compressor, Advanced Neutronium Compressor, BHC
  ?? Uses the immense power of the event horizon to compress things
  ?? No longer requires heat management to perform superdense compression
     Can create advanced singularities!
     -----------------------------------------
     Insert a Black Hole Seed to open a black hole
     The black hole will begin its life at 100 stability and slowly decay
     Stability decays by 1/s until it reaches 0
     At 0 stability, the black hole is UNSTABLE
     Once the black hole becomes unstable, it will void recipes and eventually close itself!
     -----------------------------------------
     The decay can be halted by inserting 1 L/s of spacetime into specific hatches
     Every 30 total seconds saved by spacetime insertion will double the cost per second!
     Insert a Black Hole Collapser to close the black hole
     To restore stability and reset spacetime costs, close the black hole and open a new one
     -----------------------------------------
     Use circuit 20 for Compressor and 21 for Neutronium Compressor
     8 Parallels per Voltage Tier
     500% Speed
     70% EU Usage
  ?? 2x/4x parallels when stability is BELOW 50/20
  ?? Below 20 stability, parallels are uncapped for recipes that do not require a black hole.
     Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!

### Residual Decontaminant Degasser Purification Unit  [maps: Degassing]
     Residual Decontaminant Degasser Purification Unit
     Machine Type: Purification Unit
  ?? Water Tier: 7
     Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
     -----------------------------------------
     At the start of the operation, the Degasser Control Hatch will output a redstone signal
     To succeed the recipe, you will need to successfully decode the instructions in the signal
     To decode the signal, interpret the signal strength as a 4-bit number from 0-15
     Denote the lowest bit as bit 1, and the highest as bit 4
     -----------------------------------------
     Bit 1: Ozone Sparging by Inert Gas
     If this bit is on, you must insert an inert gas into the machine
     To determine which gas to insert, interpret bits 2-3 as a 2-bit number
     0: 10000L Helium / 1: 7500L Neon / 2: 5000L Krypton / 3: 2500L Xenon
     -----------------------------------------
     Bit 2: Superconductive Deionization
     If this bit is on, you must insert 1440L Molten Superconductor Base.
  ?? Using higher tier superconductor provides bonus output
  ?? Output multiplier: UV: 1x / UHV: 1.25x / UEV: 1.5x / UIV: 1.75x / UMV: 2x
     -----------------------------------------
     Bit 3: Gravitationally-Generated Differential Vacuum Extraction
     If this bit is on, you must insert 4608L Molten Neutronium
     -----------------------------------------
     Bit 4: Seldonian Settlement Process
     If this bit is on, DISREGARD all other bits and do not insert anything
     -----------------------------------------
     No bits: Machine Overload
     In rare cases, the machine may overload and output no control signal at all
     To prevent machine damage, insert 10000L Super Coolant.
     -----------------------------------------
     The recipe can only succeed if the entire signal is decoded correctly
     Inserting any fluid not requested by the signal will always void the recipe
     -----------------------------------------
     The penultimate stage of water purification, step seven, is an irregular series of complex
     processes designed to remove any residual materials left by the decontaminants from the previous
     steps such as any energetic ions, acids, clarifiers, or gasses. Depending on what the Degasser
     detects in the water, it will request various materials to complete the processes listed above.
     -----------------------------------------
     8
     shift

### Solar Tower  [maps: Solar Tower]
     Solar Tower
     Machine Type: Solar Tower
     Contributing Green Energy towards the future
     Surround with rings of Solar Reflectors
  ?? The Reflectors increase the internal heat value of the Tower (see below for formula)
  ?? Each Reflector ring increases tier, the first ring is required for the Tower to work
     Input: Solar Salt (Cold)
     Output: Solar Salt (Hot)
  ?? Every cycle (10 seconds), heat increases and all the Cold Solar Salt is heated
  ?? Converting Cold to Hot Solar Salt reduces heat, equal to the amount converted
  ?? This conversion only happens if heat >= 30000 and controller efficiency = 100%
  ?? If there's more Cold Salt than heat, all the heat is used up and returns to 0
  ?? The heat increase is most efficient at exactly half of maximum heat
  ?? Minimum efficiency at 0 or 100000 heat, maximum efficiency at 50000
  ?? Heat Efficiency formula: ( 7000 - [|currentHeat - 50000| ^ 0.8]) / 7000
  ?? Heat gain per cycle: numberHeaters * heatEfficiency * (10 + bonus)
  ?? Bonus: 1 ring = +1, 2 rings = +2, 3 rings = +4, 4 rings = +8, 5 rings = +16
     Total number of reflectors based on how many rings are built:
     1 ring = 36, 2 rings = 88, 3 rings = 156, 4 rings = 240, 5 rings = 340
     -----------------------------------------
     shift

     and C depends on the fuel type. Diesel: C=0.04; Gas: C=0.04; Rocket fuel: C=0.005
  ?? It creates sqrt(Current Output Power) pollution every second

## All machines

### Absolute Baryonic Perfection Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: Baryonic Perfection]
    Absolute Baryonic Perfection Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 8
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    Insert Quark Releasing Catalysts into the input bus while running
    Every recipe cycle, a different combination of 2 different Quark Releasing Catalysts
    will correctly identify the lone quark and succeed the recipe
    The order of catalysts in the combination does not matter, so Up/Down can be identified by inserting Down/Up
    -----------------------------------------
    Every 20 ticks, consumes ALL catalysts in the input bus
    The base cost of inserting a catalyst is 144L Molten Infinity
    For every duplicate occurrence of an inserted catalyst in the sequence, this cost is doubled
    -----------------------------------------
    Keeps track of the entire sequence of catalysts inserted this recipe
    If the correct catalyst combination is in the sequence of inserted catalysts, immediately outputs 2000L Stabilised Baryonic Matter
    At the end of a successful recipe, outputs additional 500L Stabilised Baryonic Matter per parallel
    At the end of the recipe, returns all incorrectly inserted catalysts in the output bus
    -----------------------------------------
    The final stage of water purification goes beyond subatomic particles and identifies the smallest
    possible imperfections within the baryons themselves. By correctly identifying which pairs of quark
    flavors are required, the unit will activate the catalysts, stabilizing the errant particles
    This ultimately creates both Stabilised Baryonic Matter and, most importantly, absolutely perfectly purified water.
    -----------------------------------------
    9
    shift

### Advanced Assembly Line (single) [maps: Assemblyline Process]
    Advanced Assembly Line
    Machine Type: Assembly Line, AAL
    Assembly Line with item pipelining
    All fluids are consumed at the start of the recipe
    Recipe tier is limited by the lowest Energy Hatch tier
    -------------------------------------------------------------------
    Runs imperfect overclocks until Energy Hatch tier
    Additional overclocks are increasingly more expensive
    Multiplier = 4^(Regular Overclocks) × 4.3 × 4.6 × … × (4 + 0.3 × Extra Overclocks)
    Power usage = Multiplier × (Active Slices) × (Recipe EU/t)
    Overclocking assumes all recipe slices are active
    Does not overclock beyond 1 tick
    -------------------------------------------------------------------
    Constructed identically to the Assembly Line
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Bose-Einstein Condensate Entanglement Apparatus (multiblock) [maps: Condensate Entanglement]
    Bose-Einstein Condensate Entanglement Apparatus
    Machine Type: BEC Generator, Condensate Entangler, Input Hatch
    Converts Prepared Condensate into Entangled Condensate.
    Entangled Condensate can only be transferred via a Bose-Einstein Condensate Conduit.
    Condensate will be processed even if there is no space in the network.
    Condensate can only be stored in a Bose-Einstein Condensate Containment Field.
    -----------------------------------------
    Performs complex parallels.
    Consumes as much Prepared Condensate as possible, depending on how much EU/t is supplied.
    Machine processing time is the max duration of all active recipes.
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Recursive Pineapple
    shift

### Bose-Einstein Condensate Observation Array (multiblock) [maps: Condensate Assembling]
    Bose-Einstein Condensate Observation Array
    Machine Type: BEC Assembler, Observation Array
    Supplies nanites, power, and condensate to Observation Array Teleportation Nodes.
    Nanites must be stored in a Nanite Containment Bus.
    Different types of nanites can be mixed, but the effective tier will be the lowest of all provided nanites.
    -----------------------------------------
    Observation Array Teleportation Nodes will stall if they request more power than the observation array can provide.
    A recipe's condensate can be supplied at any time during its crafting.
    The recipe will fail if condensate is not supplied by the end.
    Crafting speed will be reduced if the network contains condensate that is not requested by the recipe.
    If there are more than 3 unrequested condensates in the network while a teleportation node is running, the craft will immediately fail.
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Recursive Pineapple
    shift

    by putting the imprint in the controller
    Every Circuit Assembly Line can only be imprinted ONCE
    -----------------------------------------

    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 1
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    Requires a filter made of Activated Carbon to work
    Every cycle, has a 20% chance to destroy the filter
    -----------------------------------------
    The first step to acquiring purified water is to filter out macroscopic contaminants through the
    use of large physical filters. As more contaminants are captured, the efficacy of the filter
    decreases so continual replacements must be supplied to maintain full function of the Clarifier.
    -----------------------------------------
    2

### Dangote Distillus (multiblock) [maps: Distillation Tower, Distillery]
    Dangote Distillus
    Machine Type: Distillery, DT
    Stats dictated by tower mode
    Right click the controller with screwdriver to change mode
    -----------------------------------------
    Distillery Mode
    Outputs only one fluid and requires max height tower
    (2 * floor(Height / 3)) * Voltage Tier Parallels
    200% Speed
    15% EU Usage
    -----------------------------------------
    Distillation Tower Mode
    Fluids are outputted one per layer based on the slot number in NEI
    Increase the height to output more fluid types
    12 Parallels
    350% Speed
    100% EU Usage
    480 Pollution per second
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Decay Warehouse (multiblock) [maps: Isotope Decay]
    Decay Warehouse
    Machine Type: Decay Warehouse
    Stores a single type of radioactive isotope and allows it to decay over time
    Decay speed is dependent on the isotopes' half-lives (lower is faster)
    Isotopes decay regardless of whether the warehouse is on or powered
    -----------------------------------------
    The warehouse's capacity equals the super chest's capacity divided by 400.
    The warehouse will pull in up to N / 512 items per second,
    where N is the warehouse's EU input (standard energy hatch rules)
    -----------------------------------------
    Right click the controller with a screwdriver to dump stored isotopes into the output bus
    Right click the controller with a plunger to empty it
    The warehouse's contents are voided when the controller is broken
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Dimensionally Transcendent Plasma Forge (multiblock) [maps: DTPF]
    Dimensionally Transcendent Plasma Forge
    Machine Type: Plasma Forge, DTPF
    Transcending Dimensional Boundaries.
    Takes 8 hours of continuous run time to fully breach dimensional
    boundaries and achieve maximum efficiency, reducing fuel consumption by up to 50%
    When no recipe is running, fuel discount decays x100 as fast as it builds up, draining
    the total amount of stored runtime
    -----------------------------------------
    Multidimensional spaces can be perfectly aligned and synchronized in this state,
    allowing Dimensional Convergence to occur. To reach the required stability threshold,
    a Transdimensional Alignment Matrix must be placed in the controller
    When Convergence is active, it allows the forge to perform Perfect Overclocks,
    but the extra power cost is instead added in form of increased catalyst amounts
    This machine can run recipes regardless of tier, if given enough energy
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Colen
    shift

### Endothermic Fridge (multiblock) [maps: Vacuum Freezer]
    Endothermic Fridge
    Machine Type: Vacuum Freezer, EnF, MVF
    256 Parallels
    -----------------------------------------
    While active, the machine will cool down and increase its speed bonus up to 1.5x
    Takes 5 minutes of constant running to reach maximum bonus
    While not running, the machine will return to normal temperatures
    Optionally supply 250 L/s of Cryotheum to speed up cooling by 5x
    The drain rate of Cryotheum will increase linearly with the speed modifier
    -----------------------------------------
    Upgrade to Tier 2 to unlock Subspace Cooling
    Will apply perfect overclocks by consuming exotic coolants:
    20 L/s : 1 : Molten Infinity
    20 L/s : 2 : Molten Spacetime
    20 L/s : 3 : Molten Eternity
    -----------------------------------------
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    This machine can run recipes regardless of tier, if given enough energy
    -----------------------------------------
    ... without the other!
    -----------------------------------------
    Structure by: Pix3lated
    shift

### Entropic Processor (multiblock) [maps: Entropic Processing]
    Entropic Processor
    Machine Type: Entropic Processor
    Processes substances by increasing or decreasing their entropy
    -----------------------------------------
    Has 8 parallels by default
    Using Advanced Alchemical Constructs increases the machine's parallels to 32
    -----------------------------------------
    Mixes fluids or solids with a magical catalyst
    Catalyst is consumed by the recipe, then returned upon completion
    -----------------------------------------
    Performs one perfect overclock per casing tier (Thaumium = 1 perfect OC)
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Exothermic Hearth (multiblock) [maps: Blast Furnace]
    Exothermic Hearth
    Machine Type: Blast Furnace, ExH, MEBF, MBF
    256 Parallels
    -5% EU Usage per 900K above the recipe requirement
    -----------------------------------------
    Increases Heat by 100K for every Voltage tier past MV
    Every 1800K over the recipe requirement grants 1 Perfect Overclock
    -----------------------------------------
    While active, the machine will heat up and multiply its parallels up to 2x
    Takes 30 minutes of constant running to reach maximum multiplier
    While not running, the machine will rapidly cooldown
    Optionally supply 250 L/s of Pyrotheum to speed up heating by 6x
    The drain rate of Pyrotheum will increase linearly with the parallel multiplier
    -----------------------------------------

    This machine can run recipes regardless of tier, if given enough energy
    102400 Pollution per second

### Extreme Combustion Engine (multiblock) [maps: Extreme Diesel Engine Fuel]
    Extreme Combustion Engine
    Machine Type: Combustion Generator, ECE

### Extreme Heat Exchanger (single) [maps: Extreme Heat Exchanger]
    Extreme Heat Exchanger
    Machine Type: Heat Exchanger, EHE
    Inputs are Lava, Hot Coolant, Hot Solar Salt, or Plasma
    Outputs are Pahoehoe Lava, IC2 Coolant, Cold Solar Salt, or Molten Metal
    Converts distilled water into SH Steam or SC Steam in the process
    Outputs SC Steam if the input rate of hot fluid is above a certain threshold
    Explodes immediately if it runs out of distilled water
    -----------------------------------------
    Lava | SC Threshold 80,000 L/s | Max Input 160,000 L/s | Max Output 640,000 L/t SC Steam
    Hot Coolant | SC Threshold 8,000 L/s | Max Input 16,000 L/s | Max Output 160,000 L/t SC Steam
    Hot Solar Salt | SC Threshold 1,600 L/s | Max Input 3,200 L/s | Max Output 160,000 L/t SC Steam
    -----------------------------------------
    Plasma always outputs Dense SC Steam regardless of input rate
    The max input and output rates depend on the plasma's density (EU/L)
    -----------------------------------------
    A circuit in the controller lowers the SC threshold at the cost of steam
    -1.5% SC Threshold and -1.5% steam output per circuit over 1
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Extreme Temperature Fluctuation Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: Temperature Fluctuation]
    Extreme Temperature Fluctuation Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 5
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    Complete heating cycles by first heating the water to 10000K,
    and then cooling it back down to 0K
    Initial temperature is reset to 0K on recipe start
    Each completed heating cycle boosts success chance by 33%
    If the temperature ever reaches 12500K the recipe will fail and output steam
    -----------------------------------------
    Consumes up to 10L/s Helium Plasma and up to 100L/s Super Coolant
    Raises the temperature by 100K per liter of plasma consumed
    Lowers the temperature by 5K per liter of coolant consumed
    -----------------------------------------
    Step five of water purification is to evaporate complex organic polymers and extremophile organisms
    that might be resistant to simple acids, clarifying agents, and filters. Using an ultra high
    pressure chamber in combination with extreme temperature fluctuations allows the water to remain
    supercritical while evaporating any remaining contaminants, ready for filtration
    -----------------------------------------
    6
    shift

### ExxonMobil Chemical Plant (multiblock) [maps: Chemical Plant]
    ExxonMobil Chemical Plant
    Machine Type: Chemical Plant
    Heavy Industry, now right at your doorstep!
    Plant tier is determined by casing tier
    Hatch tiers can't be higher than machine casing tier, UHV casing unlocks all tiers
    2 Parallels per Pipe Casing Tier
    +20% chance of not damaging catalyst per Pipe Casing Tier

    Awakened Draconium Coils combined with Tungstensteel Pipe Casings makes catalyst unbreakable
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Eye of Harmony (multiblock) [maps: Eye of Harmony]
    Eye of Harmony
    Machine Type: Spacetime Manipulator, EOH
    Creates a pocket of spacetime that is bigger on the inside using transdimensional
    engineering. Certified Time Lord regulation compliant. This multi uses too much EU
    to be handled with conventional means. All EU requirements are handled directly by
    your wireless EU network
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    This multiblock will constantly consume hydrogen and helium when it is not running a
    recipe once per second. It will store this internally, you can see the totals by
    using a scanner. This multi also has three tiered blocks with 9 tiers
    each. They are as follows and have the associated effects on the multi:
    Spacetime Compression Field Generator:
    - The tier of this block determines what recipes can be run. If the multiblocks
    spacetime compression field block exceeds the requirements of the recipe it
    will decrease the processing time by 3% per tier over the requirement (multiplicative)
    Time Dilation Field Generator:
    - Decreases the time required for a recipe by 50% per tier of block (multiplicative)
    Decreases the probability of a recipe succeeding by 9.25% per tier (additive)
    Stabilisation Field Generator:
    - Increases the probability of a recipe succeeding by 5% per tier (additive)
    Decreases the yield of a recipe by 5% per tier (additive).
    > Low tier stabilisation field generators have a power output penalty
    The power output penalty for using Crude Stabilisation Field Generators is 40%
    This penalty decreases by 5% per tier (additive)
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Going over a recipe requirement on hydrogen or helium has a penalty on yield and recipe chance
    All stored hydrogen and helium is consumed during a craft. The associated formulas are:
    Overflow ratio = (Stored fluid / Recipe requirement) - 1
    Adjustment value = 1 - exp(-(30 * Overflow ratio)^2)
    The Adjustment value is then subtracted from the total yield and recipe chance
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    It should be noted that base recipe chance is determined per recipe and yield always starts
    at 1 and subtracts depending on penalties. All fluid/item outputs are multiplied by the
    yield. Failure fluid is exempt
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    This multiblock can only output to ME output buses/hatches
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    This multiblock can be overclocked by placing a programmed circuit into the input bus
    E.g. A circuit of 2 will provide 2 OCs, 16x EU input and 0.25x the time. EU output is unaffected
    All outputs are equal. All item and fluid output chances & amounts per recipe are unaffected
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    If a recipe fails the EOH will output Success chance * 14,400 * (2.0)^(Recipe tier)L of molten
    SpaceTime instead of fluid/item outputs and output as much EU as a successful recipe
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    This multiblock can perform parallel processing by placing Astral Array Fabricators into the input bus
    They are stored internally and can be retrieved via right-clicking the controller with a wire cutter
    The maximum amount of stored Astral Arrays is 8,637. Parallel amount is calculated via these formulas:
    Parallel exponent = floor(log(8 * Astral Array amount) / log(1.7))
    Parallel = 2^(Parallel exponent)
    If the EOH is running parallel recipes, the power calculation changes
    The power needed for parallel processing is calculated as follows:
    total EU = ((EU output - EU input * 1.63) / 20.7) * 2.3^(Parallel exponent)
    Furthermore, if parallel recipes are run, the recipes consume Condensed Raw Stellar Plasma Mixture
    instead of helium and hydrogen. Overflow penalties still apply
    The required amount of fluid to start a recipe is 12.4 / 10^6 * Helium amount * Parallel
    The success or failure of each parallel is determined independently
    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Animations can be disabled by using a screwdriver on the multiblock

### Flocculation Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: Flocculation]
    Flocculation Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 3
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    Supply with Polyaluminium Chloride to operate
    Outputs Flocculation Waste Liquid that can be recycled
    -----------------------------------------
    During operation, will consume ALL Polyaluminium Chloride in the input hatch
    At the end of the recipe, for every 100000L of Polyaluminium Chloride consumed
    gain an additive 10.0% increase to success. If total fluid supplied is not
    a multiple of 100000L, a penalty to success is applied using the following formula:
    Success = Success * 2^(-10 * Overflow ratio)
    -----------------------------------------
    Step three in purifying water is to remove microscopic contaminants such as dusts, microplastics and other
    pollutants using a clarifying agent (In this case, polyaluminium chloride) to cause flocculation - the process
    of aggregating dispersed suspended particles from a solution into larger clumps for further filtration.
    -----------------------------------------
    4
    shift

### Forge of the Gods (multiblock) [maps: Godforge Upgrades]
    Forge of the Gods
    Machine Type: Stellar Forge
    Also known as Godforge or Gorge for short
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    A massive structure harnessing the thermal, gravitational and
    kinetic energy of a stabilised neutron star for material processing
    This multiblock can house up to 16 modules which utilize the star to energize materials
    to varying degrees, ranging from regular smelting to matter degeneration
    EU requirements for all modules are handled via wireless energy directly
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    This multiblock has an extensive upgrade tree which influences all of its functions,
    such as unlocking new module types, increasing heat levels and granting
    various processing speed bonuses. These upgrades can be unlocked by reaching
    certain milestones and/or spending materials
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Clicking on the logo in the controller gui opens an extensive information window,
    explaining everything there is to know about this multiblock
    -----------------------------------------
    25
    shift

### Full-Board Immersion Device (multiblock) [maps: Full-Board Immersion Device]
    Full-Board Immersion Device
    Machine Type: Board Processor, NAC Module
    Module of the Nanochip Assembly Complex
    Processes your Board CCs by cleaning them in 'Immersion Fluid'
    -----------------------------------------
    Will try to fill its internal tank with provided 'Immersion Fluids' every second
    The internal tank has a capacity of 1,000,000L and must be at least half full for the machine to work
    Running recipes gradually increases fluid impurity, with bigger amounts of fluid taking longer to become impure
    Set an ejection threshold in the GUI to automatically flush the fluid out to be processed, which is 100% by default
    -----------------------------------------
    The Impurity percentage (y%) affects the module's efficiency:
    0%-15% - EU Discount = 30% - 2 * y%
    15%-65% - Default behaviour
    65%-100% - Extra EU cost = 2% * y%
    -----------------------------------------
    Gotta get'em wet
    -----------------------------------------
    13
    shift

### High Energy Laser Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: High Energy Laser Treatment]
    High Energy Laser Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 6
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    During operation, swap the lens in the Lens Housing
    The multiblock will output a signal through the Lens Indicator Hatch
    when the current lens must be swapped
    Lens swaps will be requested in random intervals of 3 to 15s
    -----------------------------------------
    Success chance is boosted by 10.0% for each successful swap performed.
    Removing a lens too early will fail the recipe
    Find the order of lenses in the recipe in NEI,
    or use a portable scanner to view the currently requested lens
    The recipe always starts at the Orundum Lens
    -----------------------------------------
    The sixth step of water purification involves identifying any remaining negatively charged ions within
    the water which may cause electrical faults in future wafer manufacturing. Bombarding the water with varying
    wavelengths of photon beams will impart energy into outer-shell electrons, causing them to detach from the
    atoms themselves and pass through the walls of the tank, ensuring the water is perfectly electrically polar.
    -----------------------------------------
    7
    shift

### High Temperature Gas-cooled Reactor (multiblock) [maps: High Temperature Gas Reactor]
    High Temperature Gas-cooled Reactor
    Machine Type: Breeder Reactor, HTGR
    Uses up to 0.14% of fuel per operation, 10% of this value is flat and 90% dependent on the formula y=1-(1-x)^3 (x is % fill level)
    Maintenance problems decrease the efficiency of cooling by 20% for each issue
    Uses 1,536 EU/t increasing by up to 40 times when lacking Helium Gas
    Helium gas increases effectiveness of heat exchangers linearly up to 100% at max capacity
    The Reactor loses 0.05% helium per operation and requires at least 10% helium to start operation
    One Operation takes longer based on reactor fill level (between 100s and 1,000s)
    Providing coolant/water/both speeds up recipe by 7%/3%/10% total recipe time/second
    The amount of necessary fluid for maximum bonus speed scales with TRISO Balls, 0.5 coolant/tick/ball and 0.1 distilled water/tick/ball

    Added by: kuba6000 & Pxx500
    shift

### Hot Isostatic Pressurization Unit (multiblock) [maps: Compressor]
    Hot Isostatic Pressurization Unit
    Machine Type: Compressor, HIP
    HIP Unit heats up while running
    When it reaches maximum heat, it becomes overheated!
    This is only resolved by letting the machine fully cool down
    When overheated, recipes are slowed down drastically
    -----------------------------------------
    Some recipes require HIP
    If the machine is overheated when one of these recipes starts, it will be voided!
    Read the current heat using Heat Sensor Hatches
    -----------------------------------------
    More advanced coils allow better heat control - the unit will take longer to overheat
    Unit heats by (5% x 0.90 ^ (Coil Tier - 1)) every second while running
    Unit cools by 2% base every second while not running
    Cooling rate increases by an additional 2% for each second since running
    -----------------------------------------
    250% faster/slower than singleblock machines of the same voltage
    Uses 75%/110% the EU/t normally required
    Gains 4/1 parallels per voltage tier
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Ollie
    shift

### Large Hadron Collider (multiblock) [maps: Large Hadron Collider]
    Large Hadron Collider
    Machine Type: Particle Accelerator, Particle Collider, LHC
    Accelerates particles to high energies and then collides them to generate new particles
    Electrically neutral particles are unaffected
    Set the Target Beam Energy (up to 2TeV) and the maximum number of cycles
    Cycles every second
    -----------------------------------------
    Accelerator mode
    Accelerates the beam in the smaller LHC ring
    Accelerator mode increases the Beam Energy by EU Consumed in cycle * 0.1eV / Beam Rate,
    until the Target Beam Energy is reached, or the maximum number of cycles is reached
    If the Target Beam Energy is reached, add 0.3 * Beam Rate to the current Beam Rate
    However, if the current Beam Rate >= 5000, add (0.3 * Beam Rate)^0.5
    to the current Beam Rate instead
    Power cost starts at 1A of UV per Beam Rate and increases quadratically
    with the number of completed cycles
    P = (1A UV) * R * (N+1)^2
    Automatically switches to Collider mode when the maximum number of cycles is reached
    -----------------------------------------
    Collider mode
    Splits the beam into two beams that go in opposite directions in the larger LHC ring, which
    then collide to generate new particles
    The Collision Energy is two times the Beam Energy
    If the Collision Energy exceeds the rest mass of a particle, that particle will start
    appearing in the outputs
    A particle output can only appear in the modules that correspond to the forces that
    interact with that particle
    For example, Neutrinos cannot appear in the Strong force module, ALICE, since they do
    not interact via the strong force
    Filtered Beam Output Hatches will allow you to see the list of possible outputs, and
    filter them
    -----------------------------------------
    There are four LHC modules, marked by the letter on top of the module:
    E - Electromagnetism - Charged Matter Sensor (CMS)
    W - Weak Interaction - Advanced Total Lepton Assimilation Snare (ATLAS)
    S - Strong Force - Absolute Lattice Integrated Chromodynamic Encapsulator (ALICE)
    G - Gravity - Localized Horizon Curvature Binder (LHCb)
    -----------------------------------------
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Author: HamCorp
    shift

    Sodium Hydroxide : 150% : 60/minute
    Potassium Hydroxide : 190% : 24/minute
    Caesium Hydroxide : 250% : 6/minute
    Francium Hydroxide : 500% : 5/minute
    -----------------------------------------
    Produces Toxic Residue from burning acids
    If the Toxic Residue exceeds the Capacity, the multiblock will EXPLODE!
    Every tick, Toxic Residue will increase by Residue Rate*Fuel Consumption(L)*rand(0.7,1.3)
    Residue Rate is calculated as 0.05*Base Fuel Value (EU/L)^0.8
    Every tick, Toxic Residue will decrease by Base Decay*Decay Boost*(Toxic Residue^0.08)
    Residue will decay 10 times slower when multiblock is disabled
    -----------------------------------------
    Insert Robot Arms to increase Decay Boost:
    Decay Boost is calculated as 1.2^Robot Arm Tier if IV or below, 1.4^Robot Arm Tier if LuV or above
    Insert multiple (16 max) robot arms to multiply Decay Boost by sqrt(Robot Arm Amount)
    Every minute, Robot Arm Amount/(45*(1+Robot Arm Tier)) chance for one used robot arm to void
    -----------------------------------------
    Structure has 3 Tiers, Tier determines Base Decay and Capacity:
    T1 : Strengthened Inanimate Machine Casing : 200 : 375,000
    T2 : Precise Stationary Machine Casing : 400 : 1,000,000
    T3 : Ultimate Static Machine Casing : 700 : 2,500,000
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Nanochip Assembly Complex (multiblock) [maps: Nanochip Conversion Complex]
    Nanochip Assembly Complex
    Machine Type: NAC, Nanochip Assembly Complex
    Uses Circuit Components (CCs) to create circuits at unimaginable speeds
    Click the Logo Icon in the GUI for more information
    -----------------------------------------
    CCs can only exist in the perfect environment present in Vacuum Conveyor Hatches
    Convert items to CCs in the control room by placing them in a colored input bus
    Every 5 seconds, all items in colored input buses are placed
    in Vacuum Conveyor Outputs (VCOs) of the same color
    Finished circuit CCs are converted back into circuits by routing them to a VCI in the control room
    CCs in a colored VCI are placed in an output bus of the same color
    -----------------------------------------
    CCs must be processed into a refined version by modules before they
    can be assembled into a circuit in the Nanochip Assembly Matrix
    All power from the energy hatch is distributed to the modules as they need it
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Home of the Gestalt Rapidly Evolving Genesis Optimization System (GREGOS),
    a pseudo-sentient program overseeing circuit production in the NAC
    It seems to be eager to learn more, maybe you can become friends?
    -----------------------------------------
    10
    shift

### Observation Array Teleportation Node (multiblock) [maps: Condensate Assembling]
    Observation Array Teleportation Node
    Machine Type: BEC I/O Node, Input Bus, Output Bus
    Teleports items into and out of the Bose-Einstein Condensate Observation Array.
    Recipe logic is the same as all other multiblocks.
    Condensate does not have to be present for a recipe to start.
    Link it to Bose-Einstein Condensate Observation Array using Line-Of-Sight Connector Hatch.
    -----------------------------------------
    Each item slot in a recipe has an associated nanite tier.
    For the teleportation node to progress through an item slot, it must receive a nanite with the same or higher tier.
    Excess nanites linearly increase the speed at which recipes are crafted.
    This multiblock's EU/t remains constant even when it is paused, stalled, or slowed down.
    Each recipe crafted in parallel increases the EU/t consumed linearly.
    This multiblock does not overclock.
    -----------------------------------------
    The min and max parallels can be configured in the parameters to guarantee exact recipe timings.
    Recipes can be forcefully slowed down to compensate for slow automation by the Speed Divisor parameter.
    -----------------------------------------
    The Nanite Detector Hatch can be used to detect the teleportation node's requested nanites.
    The Teleportation Node Controller Hatch can be used to temporarily pause the teleportation node.
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Recursive Pineapple
    shift

### PCB Factory (multiblock) [maps: PCB Factory]
    PCB Factory
    Machine Type: Circuit Board Fabricator
    Tier of the machine determines the available recipes
    Each tier and upgrade requires additional structures
    Power consumption is multiplied by Sqrt(structures)
    Tier 2 and 3 allow parallel by using extra nanites
    Nanites have to be placed in a Nanite Containment Bus
    The formula for parallels is the amount of nanites^0.75, rounded up
    Maximum parallel is 256
    Recipes require a cooling upgrade to be overclocked
    Liquid Cooling uses 10 L/s of distilled water and enables default overclocks
    Thermosink uses 10 L/s of Super Coolant and enables perfect overclocks
    Trace size can be changed to modify the material usage and machine speed
    Configure Trace Size in UI
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: BlueWeabo
    shift

### pH Neutralization Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: pH Neutralization]
    pH Neutralization Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 4
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    Initial pH value every cycle varies from 4.5 - 9.5 pH
    If the pH value is within 0.05 pH of 7.0 pH at the end of the cycle, the recipe always succeeds
    Otherwise, the recipe always fails
    Use a pH Sensor Hatch to read the current pH value
    For safety, the machine will shut down if the pH goes below 0 or exceeds 14
    -----------------------------------------
    Every 20 ticks, consumes ALL Sodium Hydroxide and Hydrochloric Acid in the special hatches
    Raises the pH value by 0.01 pH per piece of Sodium Hydroxide Dust
    Lowers the pH value by 0.01 pH per 10L of Hydrochloric Acid
    -----------------------------------------
    The fourth step of water purification is to neutralize the solution and bring its pH to exactly 7, rendering
    the solution inert with no hydrogen ion activity beyond water’s natural amphiproticity. Acids and bases from soils
    and geology cause natural alkalinity variations in water which can cause corrosive reactions with sensitive
    materials. This necessitates the use of the corresponding neutralizing agents to pH balance the water.
    -----------------------------------------
    5
    shift

### Pseudostable Black Hole Containment Field (multiblock) [maps: Compressor, Neutronium Compressor]
    Pseudostable Black Hole Containment Field
    Machine Type: Compressor, Advanced Neutronium Compressor, BHC
    Uses the immense power of the event horizon to compress things
    No longer requires heat management to perform superdense compression
    Can create advanced singularities!
    -----------------------------------------
    Insert a Black Hole Seed to open a black hole
    The black hole will begin its life at 100 stability and slowly decay
    Stability decays by 1/s until it reaches 0
    At 0 stability, the black hole is UNSTABLE
    Once the black hole becomes unstable, it will void recipes and eventually close itself!
    -----------------------------------------
    The decay can be halted by inserting 1 L/s of spacetime into specific hatches
    Every 30 total seconds saved by spacetime insertion will double the cost per second!
    Insert a Black Hole Collapser to close the black hole
    To restore stability and reset spacetime costs, close the black hole and open a new one
    -----------------------------------------
    Use circuit 20 for Compressor and 21 for Neutronium Compressor
    8 Parallels per Voltage Tier
    500% Speed
    70% EU Usage
    2x/4x parallels when stability is BELOW 50/20
    Below 20 stability, parallels are uncapped for recipes that do not require a black hole.
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    Recipe tier is limited to hatch tier + 1. Will not perform overclocks above the hatch tier
    Limited to one energy hatch if using a Multi-Amp or Laser hatch
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: Ollie & BucketBrigade
    shift

### Quantum Force Transformer (multiblock) [maps: Quantum Force Transformer]
    Quantum Force Transformer
    Machine Type: Quantum Force Transformer, QFT
    Allows Complex processing lines to be performed instantly in one step
    -----------------------------------------
    Pulse Manipulator Tier determines maximum recipe tier
    Every recipe requires a specific catalyst
    Catalysts have to be placed in a Bulk Catalyst Housing
    Gains 1 Parallel per Catalyst
    -----------------------------------------
    Shielding Core Tier determines Focusing Bonuses
    Put a circuit in the controller to specify the focused output, based on NEI order
    If Input Separation is on: put the circuit in the circuit slot of the bus
    Consumes 4 * Focus Tier * sqrt(parallels) L Neptunium Plasma to focus
    The better the Focus Tier, the stronger the effect
    Focused Output will have its probability boosted, with other output's being reduced evenly by the total boost
    Consumes 4 * Focus Tier * sqrt(parallels) L Fermium Plasma to boost all outputs
    -----------------------------------------
    Use a screwdriver to enable Fluid mode
    Sneak + screwdriver to disable animations
    Fluid mode turns all possible outputs into their fluid variant, if avaliable
    This machine can run recipes regardless of tier, if given enough energy
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Added by: BlueWeabo & Steelux

### Residual Decontaminant Degasser Purification Unit (multiblock) [maps: Degassing]
    Residual Decontaminant Degasser Purification Unit
    Machine Type: Purification Unit
    Water Tier: 7
    Must be linked to a Purification Plant using a data stick to work
    -----------------------------------------
    At the start of the operation, the Degasser Control Hatch will output a redstone signal
    To succeed the recipe, you will need to successfully decode the instructions in the signal
    To decode the signal, interpret the signal strength as a 4-bit number from 0-15
    Denote the lowest bit as bit 1, and the highest as bit 4
    -----------------------------------------
    Bit 1: Ozone Sparging by Inert Gas
    If this bit is on, you must insert an inert gas into the machine
    To determine which gas to insert, interpret bits 2-3 as a 2-bit number
    0: 10000L Helium / 1: 7500L Neon / 2: 5000L Krypton / 3: 2500L Xenon
    -----------------------------------------
    Bit 2: Superconductive Deionization
    If this bit is on, you must insert 1440L Molten Superconductor Base.
    Using higher tier superconductor provides bonus output
    Output multiplier: UV: 1x / UHV: 1.25x / UEV: 1.5x / UIV: 1.75x / UMV: 2x
    -----------------------------------------
    Bit 3: Gravitationally-Generated Differential Vacuum Extraction
    If this bit is on, you must insert 4608L Molten Neutronium
    -----------------------------------------
    Bit 4: Seldonian Settlement Process
    If this bit is on, DISREGARD all other bits and do not insert anything
    -----------------------------------------
    No bits: Machine Overload
    In rare cases, the machine may overload and output no control signal at all
    To prevent machine damage, insert 10000L Super Coolant.
    -----------------------------------------
    The recipe can only succeed if the entire signal is decoded correctly
    Inserting any fluid not requested by the signal will always void the recipe
    -----------------------------------------
    The penultimate stage of water purification, step seven, is an irregular series of complex
    processes designed to remove any residual materials left by the decontaminants from the previous
    steps such as any energetic ions, acids, clarifiers, or gasses. Depending on what the Degasser
    detects in the water, it will request various materials to complete the processes listed above.
    -----------------------------------------
    8
    shift

### Rocketdyne F-1A Engine (multiblock) [maps: Rocket Engine Fuel]
    Rocketdyne F-1A Engine
    Machine Type: Rocket Engine
    Generating Power from Rocket Fuels
    Supply GT++ Rocket Fuels and 1000L of Carbon Dioxide per hour
    Produces as much energy as you put fuel in, with optional boosting
    This multi doesn't accept fluids if not enabled - enable it first!
    Consumes 2000L/s of air and pollutes 1500 gibbl/s per 16384 eu/t produced
    Place 1-8 Air Intake Hatches on the sides to maintain Air input
    If it runs out of air, it will shut down and have to be manually restarted
    Supply 3L of Liquid Hydrogen per second, per 1000 EU/t to boost
    Takes 3x the amount of Carbon Dioxide and maintains efficiency
    Fuel efficiency starts at ~160%, falls more slowly at higher EU/t if boosted
    If producing more than 30k EU/t, fuel efficiency will be lower:
    (These thresholds are 3x higher when boosted, boosted values displayed second)
    - 75% of max fuel efficiency at 53k or 159k EU/t output energy
    - 50% of max fuel efficiency at 69k or 207k EU/t output energy
    - 25% of max fuel efficiency at 98k or 294k EU/t output energy
    formula: x = input of energy (30000^(1/3)/ x^(1/3)) * (80000^(1/3)/ x^(1/3))
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Solar Tower (multiblock) [maps: Solar Tower]
    Solar Tower
    Machine Type: Solar Tower
    Contributing Green Energy towards the future
    Surround with rings of Solar Reflectors
    The Reflectors increase the internal heat value of the Tower (see below for formula)
    Each Reflector ring increases tier, the first ring is required for the Tower to work
    Input: Solar Salt (Cold)
    Output: Solar Salt (Hot)
    Every cycle (10 seconds), heat increases and all the Cold Solar Salt is heated
    Converting Cold to Hot Solar Salt reduces heat, equal to the amount converted
    This conversion only happens if heat >= 30000 and controller efficiency = 100%
    If there's more Cold Salt than heat, all the heat is used up and returns to 0
    The heat increase is most efficient at exactly half of maximum heat
    Minimum efficiency at 0 or 100000 heat, maximum efficiency at 50000
    Heat Efficiency formula: ( 7000 - [|currentHeat - 50000| ^ 0.8]) / 7000
    Heat gain per cycle: numberHeaters * heatEfficiency * (10 + bonus)
    Bonus: 1 ring = +1, 2 rings = +2, 3 rings = +4, 4 rings = +8, 5 rings = +16
    Total number of reflectors based on how many rings are built:
    1 ring = 36, 2 rings = 88, 3 rings = 156, 4 rings = 240, 5 rings = 340
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Source Chamber (single) [maps: Source Chamber]
    Source Chamber
    Machine Type: Particle Source
    Generates particle beams from specific source materials
    Use a portable scanner for info about the input/output of this multiblock
    -----------------------------------------
    NEI has the list of source materials and their stats
    Recipes are all one second long and output a single beam packet
    Increasing the power above the EU/t Required asymptotically approaches the Max Material Energy
    -----------------------------------------
    Particle beams have a particle type, energy, focus, and rate
    Type determines the charge, rest mass, and Max Particle Energy
    Energy is the energy of the particle beam in electron volts (eV)
    Focus is the width of the particle beam
    Rate is the amount of particles per packet
    -----------------------------------------
    Output Beam Energy = min(Max Particle Energy, aeV)
    where aeV = Max Material Energy * (1 - 1.001^(-Energy Ratio * (EU/t Provided - EU/t Required)))
    -----------------------------------------
    shift

### Tree Growth Simulator (multiblock) [maps: Tree Growth Simulator]
    Tree Growth Simulator
    Machine Type: Tree Farm, TGS
    Farms and harvests trees using EU
    Place a sapling in the controller slot
    Place a tool in an input bus
    Different tools are required for different outputs
    Advanced tools multiply output amount
    Logs: Saw (1x), Buzzsaw (2x), Chainsaw (4x)
    Saplings: Branch Cutter (1x), Grafter (4x)
    Leaves: Shears (1x), Wire Cutter (2x), Electric Wire Cutter (4x)
    Fruit: Knife (1x)
    Multiple tools can be used at the same time
    -----------------------------------------
    Work time is fixed at 5 seconds
    Energy input tier multiplies output further
    Output multiplier is equal to: 2*tier^2 - 2*tier + 5
    100 Pollution per second
    -----------------------------------------
    Structure by: EvgenWarGold
    shift

### Universal Chemical Fuel Engine (multiblock) [maps: Combustion Generator Fuels, Gas Turbine Fuel, Rocket Engine Fuel]
    Universal Chemical Fuel Engine
    Machine Type: Chemical Engine, UCFE
    BURNING BURNING BURNING
    Use combustible liquid to generate power
    You need to supply Combustion Promoter to keep it running
    It will consume all the fuel and combustion promoter in the hatch every second
    Energy output to the dynamo will be distributed over the next second
    If the Dynamo Hatch's buffer fills up, the machine will stop
    If the amount of energy to be produced is higher than the dynamo hatch can handle then all produced energy will void
    When turned on, there is a 10-second period where the machine will not stop
    Even if it doesn't stop, all the fuel in the hatch will be consumed
    The efficiency is determined by the proportion of Combustion Promoter to fuel
    The higher the amount of promoter, the higher the efficiency
    Follows an exponential curve exp(-C/(p/x))*1.5, where x is the amount of fuel in liters, p is the amount of promoter in liters
    and C depends on the fuel type. Diesel: C=0.04; Gas: C=0.04; Rocket fuel: C=0.005
    It creates sqrt(Current Output Power) pollution every second
    If you forget to supply Combustion Promoter, this engine will swallow all the fuel without outputting energy
    The efficiency is up to 150%
    Supports Multi-Amp and Laser Hatches!
    -----------------------------------------
    Structure by: TimTems
    shift
